NI = non-inferior at margin 0.02. mde80 = smallest true effect this cell's n_seeds/variance
could detect at 80% power, alpha=0.05 two-sided -- a non-significant row with small mde80 is
evidence of a small true effect; with large mde80 it is just underpowered, not evidence of none.

How to read this report: only Holm-significant rows are evidence of a difference; only NI=yes rows are evidence of (near-)equivalence at the stated margin; mde80 tells you whether a non-significant row was actually underpowered. v13_vs_random / v13_vs_magnitude / v13_vs_magnitude_rigl (sparsity-matched) test whether the movement+RigL+distill machinery beats, respectively, trivial pruning, plain magnitude, and magnitude+regrow at the same nominal sparsity; the *_gatematched versions test the same questions at matched circuit cost, which is the more meaningful comparison under noise, and are the ones the adaptive seed budget targets. regrow_effect / regrow_effect_magnitude isolate RigL regrowth's own contribution for the movement and magnitude criteria respectively -- if both are positive and similar in size, regrowth (not criterion choice) is doing the work. pruning_cost_given_qat isolates pruning from quantization; quantization_cost isolates quantization. Two-qubit-noise-ratio robustness: compare the mult column across rows of the same contrast to see whether an effect strengthens under the more realistic (higher-mult) noise model.
See *_results.csv, *_aggregate.csv, *_contrasts_<metric>.csv, *_gatematched_<metric>.csv, *_sanity_baseline.csv.
